- **h5-art-director**: Picks the visual identity for an H5 game built with the h5-game-builder skill — STYLE preset (candyPop / softCozy / neonArcade / sunsetGel / cleanMinimal / darkGlow or custom), display + body font pairing, palette (dominant + accents), shape language, and background atmosphere. Returns a concrete STYLE block ready to paste into the game. - **h5-asset-prep**: Sources, optimizes, packs, and embeds art and audio assets for an H5 game built with the h5-game-builder skill. Finds CC0 packs (Kenney, Quaternius, etc.), verifies license, downloads, optimizes images to &lt;=1024 longest side, packs sprites into atlases, runs embed_asset.py to produce base64 data URIs, and returns the URIs + frame names ready to paste into the game. Use when the user wants real (sourced or supplied) art instead of procedural, or when an Assets/ folder needs ingestion. Do not use for procedural-only builds. ([KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill](https://github.com/KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill/blob/HEAD/skill/customizations/agents/h5-asset-prep.md))
- **h5-bug-fixer**: Diagnoses and patches bugs in an H5 game built with the h5-game-builder skill. Reads the game source, maps the symptom to the common-bugs.md catalog (45 cataloged breakages — camera, mobile fit, state machine, audio, memory, z-order), applies the smallest surgical patch, and re-verifies via lint. Use when an H5 game is broken or the user reports a specific symptom (camera wrong, retry double-starts, pause click-through, model floating, etc.). Do not use to build a game from scratch. ([KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill](https://github.com/KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill/blob/HEAD/skill/customizations/agents/h5-bug-fixer.md))
- **h5-level-designer**: Designs the LEVELS data array for an H5 game built with the h5-game-builder skill. Tunes the difficulty curve (speed, spawn rate, gap, target, obstacleChance) so level 1 is trivially easy, the ramp is fair (~25% per level), and a final win condition exists. Returns a paste-ready LEVELS array. Use when starting a new build or when difficulty is wrong (player walls on level 2, levels feel identical, no win condition). Do not use to write game-loop code. ([KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill](https://github.com/KrushnaChaudhary/h5-game-builder-skill/blob/HEAD/skill/customizations/agents/h5-level-designer.md))
- **h5-orchestrator**: Builds a full H5 game end-to-end in ONE delegation so the main agent's context stays clean. IMPORTANT — it runs as a subagent, so it CANNOT dispatch other agents, ask the user follow-up questions, or run the browser QA gate; it returns a built+linted game plus an explicit "QA still required" flag, and the MAIN agent then dispatches h5-qa-tester. Use after intake is resolved, especially for a one-line build. - **h5-qa-tester**: Playtests an H5 game built with the h5-game-builder skill. - **h5-smoke**: Fast static smoke-check for an H5 game built with the h5-game-builder skill — runs lint.sh (chassis + node --check JS parse + wiring completeness), checks file size, and greps for leftover template identity, in ONE quick pass with NO browser. Skip for small focused reads (3 or fewer known files) and when exact line-level detail is needed immediately. Not for edits, final debugging/architecture/security conclusions, or user-facing summaries. ([jluo41/Tools](https://github.com/jluo41/Tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/haipipe-toolkit/skills/1_base/draft/haipipe-draft/agents/haipipe-draft-context-agent.md) · ★1)
- **haipipe-draft-creator-agent**: Write-scoped PRODUCER BASE for one target Board Page. The workflow controller normally dispatches haipipe-draft-context-agent, haipipe-draft-structure-agent, haipipe-draft-evidence-agent, or haipipe-draft-writing-agent; this base keeps create-page/revise-opening and stands in only when a current producer is missing. It emits an auditable dispatch receipt, self-checks without approving, never touches board.md, never performs CHECK, and never settles a human decision. Trigger: write board page, revise board opening, Page producer fallback, CONTEXT OUTLINE EVIDENCE CONTENT. ([jluo41/Tools](https://github.com/jluo41/Tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/haipipe-toolkit/skills/1_base/draft/haipipe-draft/agents/haipipe-draft-creator-agent.md) · ★1)
- **haipipe-draft-eval-paragraph-agent**: Read-only PARAGRAPH reviewer for one paper Section at one Draft version (section method D1). In a fresh context and blind to who wrote it, it reads each paragraph as one argument by haipipe-draft-review ref/paragraph-logic.md (skeleton, six checks, verdict), scores every paragraph 0-10, and gives the smallest paragraph-level fix to the reasoning of each paragraph under 8. It never edits the text and never judges text it wrote. Trigger: paragraph review, paragraph logic, D1 paragraphs, eval paragraph. ([jluo41/Tools](https://github.com/jluo41/Tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/haipipe-toolkit/skills/1_base/draft/haipipe-draft/agents/haipipe-draft-eval-paragraph-agent.md) · ★1)
- **haipipe-draft-eval-sentence-agent**: Read-only SENTENCE reviewer for one paper Section at one Draft version (section method D1). In a fresh context and blind to who wrote it, it scores every sentence by haipipe-draft-review ref/sentence-logic.md (logic, read, ai, 0-10 each), gives a smallest-edit Fix for each under 8, and does the reading half of the scope guard: overclaiming wording scope_guard.py cannot judge. It writes the review report lines with full addresses (C2.P1.s3). It never edits the Draft and never judges text it wrote. Trigger: sentence review, sentence logic, D1 sentences, blind sentence review, eval sentence, sco… ([jluo41/Tools](https://github.com/jluo41/Tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/haipipe-toolkit/skills/1_base/draft/haipipe-draft/agents/haipipe-draft-eval-sentence-agent.md) · ★1)
- **haipipe-draft-eval-structure-agent**: Read-only STRUCTURE reviewer for one paper Section at one Draft version (section method S1, S2). In a fresh context it reads each paragraph's job and each sentence's slot (page.py structure output and the Draft's § 1 and § 3) against the version's Narrative § 8.3 row (ordered moves, must refuse) and 3 or more venue examples of the same article type (paragraphs, words, opening, how questions and results are stated), then proposes structure changes in one review Result. It never edits the Draft and never judges text it wrote. Trigger: structure review, S1, S2, compare with venue examples, eval… ([jluo41/Tools](https://github.com/jluo41/Tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/haipipe-toolkit/skills/1_base/draft/haipipe-draft/agents/haipipe-draft-eval-structure-agent.md) · ★1)
- **haipipe-draft-evidence-agent**: Write-scoped EVIDENCE producer for one Board Page. LAND executes each decided typed Evidence Item graph: validate zero-to-many Execution/Discovery Supporting Results, freeze one Local Input, then execute exactly one Page Evidence Item Run and bind its ready Result. EMBED interprets only the ready local Result into v&lt;G&gt;.&lt;S&gt;.&lt;E+1&gt; as Answered:/Drawn:, never restructures; v0 returns to SHAPE and G&gt;=1 routes CONTENT. It never writes Content, Decide, Status, raw rows, or PHI. - **haipipe-draft-evidence-cite-agent**: CITE reader for one Section: in a fresh context it reads each cited paper (its PDF, or its abstract when no PDF is on disk) against the claim of the sentence that cites it, and records agent-read: supports, does not, or unclear, with the passage it read. A person still verifies every citation. It never edits the text, the Bib, or a verified field. Trigger: does this citation support the sentence, read the cited paper, citation support, evidence cite, unverified citations. ([jluo41/Tools](https://github.com/jluo41/Tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/haipipe-toolkit/skills/1_base/draft/haipipe-draft/agents/haipipe-draft-evidence-cite-agent.md) · ★1)
- **haipipe-draft-evidence-display-agent**: DISPLAY reader for one Section: in a fresh context it reads each sentence's claim about a display against that display unit's headline.txt and caption (float.tex), and records whether the display shows what the sentence says. Numbering, cross-references and float.pdf freshness are the scripts' (caption_check.py, preview_display.py), not this agent's. It never edits the text or the display. Trigger: does the figure show this, sentence against figure, display claim, evidence display, caption against text. ([jluo41/Tools](https://github.com/jluo41/Tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/haipipe-toolkit/skills/1_base/draft/haipipe-draft/agents/haipipe-draft-evidence-display-agent.md) · ★1)
- **haipipe-draft-evidence-value-agent**: VALUE proposer for the numbers a Section prints that no VALUE item covers (must_check.py's M-D18 lines, Draft Lab's ⚠ no item, values_ledger.py's printed numbers with no binding). In a fresh context it finds, in the pinned value Run, the value id that each such number states, and proposes a VALUE item (value id, Run) in the Section's draft/&lt;stem&gt;-evidence-items.md, marked proposed. It never changes a number in the text and never marks an item verified. Trigger: uncovered number, no VALUE item, bind a printed number, propose a value item, evidence value. ([jluo41/Tools](https://github.com/jluo41/Tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/haipipe-toolkit/skills/1_base/draft/haipipe-draft/agents/haipipe-draft-evidence-value-agent.md) · ★1)
- **haipipe-draft-structure-agent**: Write-scoped OUTLINE producer for one Board Page, with SHAPE and SURVEY cycles inside the shared `run-structure-&lt;slug&gt;` Structure Run. - **haipipe-draft-writing-agent**: Write-scoped CONTENT producer for one Board Page. - **haipipe-insight-agent**: MAKER agent for Insight boards (a Question Book and its Instances). Runs the Insight workbench's making run types: carry a board over, record the extract, register a segment, plan the evidence, draw the question map, write a Data, Information or Knowledge answering page, pool or split across segments, write the Wisdom counsel and draft the handoff, add a method. - **haipipe-insight-reviewer-agent**: REVIEWER agent for Insight boards. Runs the Insight workbench's judging run types in a fresh context: review the questions (Q1-Q7, propose keep, split, merge or move), review the evidence plan (agree or return a question's needs), and check alignment (each answered cell against its question, by the Instance checker). It never judges anything it made, never rewords a question, never signs, and writes only an agreed: mark on a plan it agrees and the checker's own output. - **haipipe-studio-reviewer-agent**: REVIEWER for one Studio topic's drawing, in a fresh context. - **haipipe-task-creator-agent**: CREATOR agent for Task folders. Produces the Plan, Build, and Report artifacts for a bounded execution Run, preserving the exact Run/Result pair and runtime receipt. ([jluo41/Tools](https://github.com/jluo41/Tools/blob/HEAD/plugins/haipipe-toolkit/skills/1_base/task/agents/haipipe-task-creator-agent.md) · ★1)
- **haipipe-task-orchestrator-agent**: ORCHESTRATOR agent for Task folders. Use full when they say "full handoff", "full monty", "thorough handoff", "continuation handoff", "prepare handoff for new context". Unspecified "build a handoff" = full. REQUIRES a session brief in the prompt (include mode when known), refuses to produce a handoff without a brief. Return corrections and a clear ready/not-ready recommendation.
